floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, \n                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, \n                   const size_t numNodes) {\n    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm\n    // For each intermediate node k\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {\n        // For each source node i\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n            // For each destination node j\n            for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {\n                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];\n                \n                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + distKJ;\n                \n                if (newDist < distIJ) {\n                    dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = newDist;\n                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {\n    // Basic sanity checks\n    \n    // 1. Diagonal should be zero\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\\n\", i, i);\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]\n    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {\n                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];\n                \n                // Check for overflow before addition\n                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {\n                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {\n                        printf(\"Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\\n\", \n                               i, j, k);\n                        return false;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t numNodes = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Number of nodes: %zu\\n\", numNodes);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    \n    // Allocate matrices\n    std::vector<unsigned int> dist(numNodes * numNodes);\n    std::vector<unsigned int> path(numNodes * numNodes);\n    \n    // Initialize\n    printf(\"Initializing graph...\\n\");\n    initializeDistanceMatrix(dist, numNodes, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);\n    initializePathMatrix(path, numNodes);\n    \n    // Run Floyd-Warshall\n    printf(\"Computing shortest paths...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    \n    floydWarshall(dist, path, numNodes);\n    \n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n    \n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n    \n    // Calculate operations per second\n    // Floyd-Warshall has O(n³) complexity\n    double ops = (double)numNodes * numNodes * numNodes;\n    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f GOPS\\n\", gflops);\n    \n    // Print results for external validation (integer hash-based)\n    if (printResults) {\n        print_results_int(dist, \"DistanceMatrix\");\n    }\n    \n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        bool valid = validateResult(dist, numNodes);\n        \n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return 0;\n}\ncmake_minimum_required(VERSION i < numNodes; ++i) {\n        unsigned int* __restrict row = data + i * numNodes;\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {\n            row[j] = 0;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nvoid initializeDistanceMatrix(unsigned int* __restrict dist, const size_t numNodes,\n                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;\n\n    // Kept sequential: rand_r carries a serial dependency through its seed, so\n    // this reproduces the exact same graph as the original code.\n    for (size_t i = 0; One parallel region is opened for the whole\n    // computation; the implicit barrier of the worksharing loop separates the\n    // k iterations.\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        // For each intermediate node k\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {\n            const unsigned int* __restrict distK = dist + k * numNodes;\n\n            // For each source node i\n            #pragma omp for schedule(static)\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n                unsigned int* __restrict distI = dist + i * numNodes;\n                unsigned int* __restrict pathI = path + i * numNodes;\n                const unsigned int distIK = distI[k];\n                const unsigned int kVal = (unsigned int)k;\n\n                // For each destination node j (branch-free so it vectorizes)\n                #pragma omp simd\n                for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {\n                    const unsigned int distIJ = distI[j];\n                    const unsigned int newDist = distIK + distK[j];\n                    const bool better = newDist < distIJ;\n\n                    distI[j] = better ? newDist : distIJ;\n                    pathI[j] = better ? kVal : pathI[j];\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const unsigned int* dist, const size_t numNodes) {\n    // Basic sanity checks\n\n    // 1. One parallel region is opened for the whole\n    // computation; the implicit barrier of the worksharing loop separates the\n    // k iterations.\n    #pragma omp parallel proc_bind(spread)\n    {\n        // For each intermediate node k\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {\n            const unsigned int* __restrict distK = dist + k * numNodes;\n\n            // For each source node i\n            #pragma omp for schedule(static)\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n                unsigned int* __restrict distI = dist + i * numNodes;\n                unsigned int* __restrict pathI = path + i * numNodes;\n                const unsigned int distIK = distI[k];\n                const unsigned int kVal = (unsigned int)k;\n\n                // For each destination node j (branch-free so it vectorizes)\n                #pragma omp simd\n                for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {\n                    const unsigned int distIJ = distI[j];\n                    const unsigned int newDist = distIK + distK[j];\n                    const bool better = newDist < distIJ;\n\n                    distI[j] = better ? newDist : distIJ;\n                    pathI[j] = better ? kVal : pathI[j];\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}","new_string":"// Block size of the tiled Floyd-Warshall. A tile of BLOCK x BLOCK unsigned ints\n// is 16 KiB, so the three tiles a block update touches stay resident in L2.\nconstexpr size_t BLOCK = 64;\n\n// Relax all pairs (i, j) of a tile through all intermediate nodes k of a block.\n// The k loop is the outermost one and the updates are done in place, which is\n// exactly the dependency structure the blocked Floyd-Warshall requires (the\n// three tiles may partially or fully overlap).\nstatic inline void floydWarshallTile(unsigned int* dist, unsigned int* path, const size_t numNodes,\n                                     const size_t iStart, const size_t iEnd,\n                                     const size_t jStart, const size_t jEnd,\n                                     const size_t kStart, const size_t kEnd) {\n    for (size_t k = kStart; k < kEnd; ++k) {\n        const unsigned int* distK = dist + k * numNodes;\n        const unsigned int kVal = (unsigned int)k;\n\n        for (size_t i = iStart; i < iEnd; ++i) {\n            unsigned int* distI = dist + i * numNodes;\n            unsigned int* pathI = path + i * numNodes;\n            const unsigned int distIK = distI[k];\n\n            // For each destination node j (branch-free so it vectorizes).\n            // Rows i and k may be the same row, but then dist[k][k] == 0 makes\n            // every update a no-op, so the iterations stay independent.\n            #pragma omp simd\n            for (size_t j = jStart; j < jEnd; ++j) {\n                const unsigned int distIJ = distI[j];\n                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + distK[j];\n                const bool better = newDist < distIJ;\n\n                distI[j] = better ? newDist : distIJ;\n                pathI[j] = better ? kVal : pathI[j];\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nvoid floydWarshall(unsigned int* dist,\n                   unsigned int* path,\n                   const size_t numNodes) {\n    // Blocked Floyd-Warshall: the k loop is split into blocks and every block\n    // step consists of three phases (diagonal tile, the row/column panels of\n    // the diagonal tile, and all remaining tiles). This computes the same\n    // distance matrix as the classic triple loop, but reuses each tile BLOCK\n    // times while it is in cache and needs only three barriers per block step\n    // instead of one per k.\n    const size_t numBlocks = (numNodes + BLOCK - 1) / BLOCK;\n\n    #pragma omp parallel proc_bind(spread)\n    {\n        for (size_t kb = 0; kb < numBlocks; ++kb) {\n            const size_t kStart = kb * BLOCK;\n            const size_t kEnd = std::min(kStart + BLOCK, numNodes);\n\n            // Phase 1: the diagonal tile depends on itself and is done serially.\n            #pragma omp single\n            {\n                floydWarshallTile(dist, path, numNodes, kStart, kEnd, kStart, kEnd, kStart, kEnd);\n            }\n\n            // Phase 2: the tiles sharing rows or columns with the diagonal tile.\n            #pragma omp for schedule(static)\n            for (size_t t = 0; t < 2 * numBlocks; ++t) {\n                const size_t b = t >= numBlocks ? t - numBlocks : t;\n                if (b == kb) {\n                    continue;\n                }\n                const size_t bStart = b * BLOCK;\n                const size_t bEnd = std::min(bStart + BLOCK, numNodes);\n\n                if (t < numBlocks) {\n                    // Row panel: tile (kb, b)\n                    floydWarshallTile(dist, path, numNodes, kStart, kEnd, bStart, bEnd, kStart, kEnd);\n                } else {\n                    // Column panel: tile (b, kb)\n                    floydWarshallTile(dist, path, numNodes, bStart, bEnd, kStart, kEnd, kStart, kEnd);\n                }\n            }\n\n            // Phase 3: all remaining tiles are independent.\n            #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n            for (size_t ib = 0; ib < numBlocks; ++ib) {\n                for (size_t jb = 0; jb < numBlocks; ++jb) {\n                    if (ib == kb || jb == kb) {\n                        continue;\n                    }\n                    const size_t iStart = ib * BLOCK;\n                    const size_t iEnd = std::min(iStart + BLOCK, numNodes);\n                    const size_t jStart = jb * BLOCK;\n                    const size_t jEnd = std::min(jStart + BLOCK, numNodes);\n\n                    floydWarshallTile(dist, path, numNodes, iStart, iEnd, jStart, jEnd, kStart, kEnd);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":376,"cache_read_input_tokens":41557,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":376},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"6442b659-7b0d-401c-8a13-9be36c0297f9","uuid":"979964e6-3fa4-47a1-ab49-71f85a24c776","timestamp":"2026-09-06T20:10:52.381Z","request_id":"req_011CenntBbTmJznnGMAGYfwa"}

Rows are distributed over the threads with a static\n// schedule so that every thread keeps working on the same rows for all k\n// iterations, which maximizes cache reuse and (together with the parallel\n// first-touch initialization below) keeps the data NUMA-local.\nstatic void firstTouch(unsigned int* __restrict data, const size_t numNodes) {\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) proc_bind(spread)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n        unsigned int* __restrict row = data + i * numNodes;\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {\n            row[j] = 0;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nvoid initializeDistanceMatrix(unsigned int* __restrict dist, const size_t numNodes,\n                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;\n\n    // Kept sequential: rand_r carries a serial dependency through its seed, so\n    // this reproduces the exact same graph as the original code.\n    for (size_t i = 0; t - numBlocks : t;","+                if (b == kb) {","+                    continue;","+                }","+                const size_t bStart = b * BLOCK;","+                const size_t bEnd = std::min(bStart + BLOCK, numNodes);"," ","-                // For each destination node j (branch-free so it vectorizes)","-                #pragma omp simd","-                for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {","-                    const unsigned int distIJ = distI[j];","-                    const unsigned int newDist = distIK + distK[j];","-                    const bool better = newDist < distIJ;","+                if (t < numBlocks) {","+                    // Row panel: tile (kb, b)","+                    floydWarshallTile(dist, path, numNodes, kStart, kEnd, bStart, bEnd, kStart, kEnd);","+                } else {","+                    // Column panel: tile (b, kb)","+                    floydWarshallTile(dist, path, numNodes, bStart, bEnd, kStart, kEnd, kStart, kEnd);","+                }","+            }"," ","-                    distI[j] = better ? Rows are distributed over the threads with a static\n// schedule so that every thread keeps working on the same rows for all k\n// iterations, which maximizes cache reuse and (together with the parallel\n// first-touch initialization below) keeps the data NUMA-local.\nstatic void firstTouch(unsigned int* __restrict data, const size_t numNodes) {\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) proc_bind(spread)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n        unsigned int* __restrict row = data + i * numNodes;\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {\n            row[j] = 0;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nvoid initializeDistanceMatrix(unsigned int* __restrict dist, const size_t numNodes,\n                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;\n\n    // Kept sequential: rand_r carries a serial dependency through its seed, so\n    // this reproduces the exact same graph as the original code.\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes * numNodes; ++i) {\n        dist[i] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);\n    }\n\n    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n        dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] = 0;\n    }\n}\n\nvoid initializePathMatrix(unsigned int* __restrict path, const size_t numNodes) {\n    // The original nested loops assign path[idx2(i, j)] = j and path[idx2(j, i)] = i,\n    // i.e. every element of the row starting at j * numNodes ends up holding j.\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) proc_bind(spread)\n    for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {\n        unsigned int* __restrict row = path + j * numNodes;\n        const unsigned int value = (unsigned int)j;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n            row[i] = value;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Block size of the tiled Floyd-Warshall. A tile of BLOCK x BLOCK unsigned ints\n// is 16 KiB, so the three tiles a block update touches stay resident in L2.\nconstexpr size_t BLOCK = 64;\n\n// Relax all pairs (i, j) of a tile through all intermediate nodes k of a block.\n// The k loop is the outermost one and the updates are done in place, which is\n// exactly the dependency structure the blocked Floyd-Warshall requires (the\n// three tiles may partially or fully overlap).\nstatic inline void floydWarshallTile(unsigned int* __restrict dist, unsigned int* __restrict path, const size_t numNodes,\n                                     const size_t iStart, const size_t iEnd,\n                                     const size_t jStart, const size_t jEnd,\n                                     const size_t kStart, const size_t kEnd) {\n    for (size_t k = kStart; k < kEnd; ++k) {\n        const unsigned int* distK = dist + k * numNodes;\n        const unsigned int kVal = (unsigned int)k;\n\n        for (size_t i = iStart; i < iEnd; ++i) {\n            unsigned int* distI = dist + i * numNodes;\n            unsigned int* pathI = path + i * numNodes;\n            const unsigned int distIK = distI[k];\n\n            // For each destination node j (branch-free so it vectorizes).\n            // Rows i and k may be the same row, but then dist[k][k] == 0 makes\n            // every update a no-op, so the iterations stay independent.\n            #pragma omp simd\n            for (size_t j = jStart; j < jEnd; ++j) {\n                const unsigned int distIJ = distI[j];\n                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + distK[j];\n                const bool better = newDist < distIJ;\n\n                distI[j] = better ? newDist : distIJ;\n                pathI[j] = better ? kVal : pathI[j];\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nvoid floydWarshall(unsigned int* dist,\n                   unsigned int* path,\n                   const size_t numNodes) {\n    // Blocked Floyd-Warshall: the k loop is split into blocks and every block\n    // step consists of three phases (diagonal tile, the row/column panels of\n    // the diagonal tile, and all remaining tiles). This computes the same\n    // distance matrix as the classic triple loop, but reuses each tile BLOCK\n    // times while it is in cache and needs only three barriers per block step\n    // instead of one per k.\n    const size_t numBlocks = (numNodes + BLOCK - 1) / BLOCK;\n\n    #pragma omp parallel proc_bind(spread)\n    {\n        for (size_t kb = 0; kb < numBlocks; ++kb) {\n            const size_t kStart = kb * BLOCK;\n            const size_t kEnd = std::min(kStart + BLOCK, numNodes);\n\n            // Phase 1: the diagonal tile depends on itself and is done serially.\n            #pragma omp single\n            {\n                floydWarshallTile(dist, path, numNodes, kStart, kEnd, kStart, kEnd, kStart, kEnd);\n            }\n\n            // Phase 2: the tiles sharing rows or columns with the diagonal tile.\n            #pragma omp for schedule(static)\n            for (size_t t = 0; t < 2 * numBlocks; ++t) {\n                const size_t b = t >= numBlocks ? t - numBlocks : t;\n                if (b == kb) {\n                    continue;\n                }\n                const size_t bStart = b * BLOCK;\n                const size_t bEnd = std::min(bStart + BLOCK, numNodes);\n\n                if (t < numBlocks) {\n                    // Row panel: tile (kb, b)\n                    floydWarshallTile(dist, path, numNodes, kStart, kEnd, bStart, bEnd, kStart, kEnd);\n                } else {\n                    // Column panel: tile (b, kb)\n                    floydWarshallTile(dist, path, numNodes, bStart, bEnd, kStart, kEnd, kStart, kEnd);\n                }\n            }\n\n            // Phase 3: all remaining tiles are independent.\n            #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n            for (size_t ib = 0; ib < numBlocks; ++ib) {\n                for (size_t jb = 0; jb < numBlocks; ++jb) {\n                    if (ib == kb || jb == kb) {\n                        continue;\n                    }\n                    const size_t iStart = ib * BLOCK;\n                    const size_t iEnd = std::min(iStart + BLOCK, numNodes);\n                    const size_t jStart = jb * BLOCK;\n                    const size_t jEnd = std::min(jStart + BLOCK, numNodes);\n\n                    floydWarshallTile(dist, path, numNodes, iStart, iEnd, jStart, jEnd, kStart, kEnd);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const unsigned int* dist, const size_t numNodes) {\n    // Basic sanity checks\n\n    // 1. Diagonal should be zero\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\\n\", i, i);\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]\n    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {\n                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];\n\n                // Check for overflow before addition\n                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {\n                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {\n                        printf(\"Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\\n\",\n                               i, j, k);\n                        return false;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t numNodes = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs newDist : distIJ;","                 pathI[j] = better ? kVal : pathI[j];","+                distI2[j] = better2 ? newDist2 : distIJ2;","+                pathI2[j] = better2 ? kVal : pathI2[j];","             }","         }","+","+        if (i < iEnd) {","+            unsigned int* __restrict distI = dist + i * numNodes;","+            const unsigned int distIK = distI[k];","+            unsigned int* __restrict pathI = path + i * numNodes + jStart;","+            distI += jStart;","+","+            FW_ROW_UPDATE(distI, pathI, distK, distIK, kVal, jLen)","+        }","     }"," }"," ","+// Dispatch to the compile time sized kernel whenever the tile is a full block.","+static inline void floydWarshallTileDispatch(unsigned int* __restrict dist, unsigned int* __restrict path,","+                                             const size_t numNodes,","+                                             const size_t iStart, const size_t iEnd,","+                                             const size_t jStart, const size_t jEnd,","+                                             const size_t kStart, const size_t kEnd) {","+    if (jEnd - jStart == BLOCK) {","+        floydWarshallTile<BLOCK>(dist, path, numNodes, iStart, iEnd, jStart, jEnd, kStart, kEnd);","+    } else {","+        floydWarshallTile<0>(dist, path, numNodes, iStart, iEnd, jStart, jEnd, kStart, kEnd);","+    }","+}","+"," void floydWarshall(unsigned int* dist,","                    unsigned int* path,","                    const size_t numNodes) {"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

Rows are distributed over the threads with a static\n// schedule so that every thread keeps working on the same rows for all k\n// iterations, which maximizes cache reuse and (together with the parallel\n// first-touch initialization below) keeps the data NUMA-local.\nstatic void firstTouch(unsigned int* __restrict data, const size_t numNodes) {\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) proc_bind(spread)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n        unsigned int* __restrict row = data + i * numNodes;\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {\n            row[j] = 0;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nvoid initializeDistanceMatrix(unsigned int* __restrict dist, const size_t numNodes,\n                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;\n\n    // Kept sequential: rand_r carries a serial dependency through its seed, so\n    // this reproduces the exact same graph as the original code.\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes * numNodes; ++i) {\n        dist[i] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);\n    }\n\n    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n        dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] = 0;\n    }\n}\n\nvoid initializePathMatrix(unsigned int* __restrict path, const size_t numNodes) {\n    // The original nested loops assign path[idx2(i, j)] = j and path[idx2(j, i)] = i,\n    // i.e. every element of the row starting at j * numNodes ends up holding j.\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) proc_bind(spread)\n    for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {\n        unsigned int* __restrict row = path + j * numNodes;\n        const unsigned int value = (unsigned int)j;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n            row[i] = value;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Block size of the tiled Floyd-Warshall. A tile of BLOCK x BLOCK unsigned ints\n// is 16 KiB, so the three tiles a block update touches stay resident in L2.\nconstexpr size_t BLOCK = 64;\n\n// Relax all pairs (i, j) of a tile through all intermediate nodes k of a block.\n// The k loop is the outermost one and the updates are done in place, which is\n// exactly the dependency structure the blocked Floyd-Warshall requires (the\n// three tiles may partially or fully overlap).\n// Relax one row against the row of the intermediate node (branch-free so that it\n// vectorizes into compares plus blends). Rows i and k may be the same row, but\n// then dist[k][k] == 0 makes every update a no-op, so the iterations of the loop\n// stay independent.\n#define FW_ROW_UPDATE(distI, pathI, distK, distIK, kVal, jLen)          \\\n    _Pragma(\"omp simd\")                                                 \\\n    for (size_t j = 0; j < (jLen); ++j) {                               \\\n        const unsigned int distIJ = (distI)[j];                         \\\n        const unsigned int newDist = (distIK) + (distK)[j];             \\\n        const bool better = newDist < distIJ;                           \\\n        (distI)[j] = better ? newDist : distIJ;                         \\\n        (pathI)[j] = better ? (kVal) : (pathI)[j];                      \\\n    }\n\n// JLEN is the compile time width of the tile (0 selects the runtime width used\n// for the remainder tiles of a matrix whose size is not a multiple of BLOCK).\n// Two rows i are processed at a time so that the loads of the k row are shared.\ntemplate <size_t JLEN>\nstatic inline void floydWarshallTile(unsigned int* __restrict dist, unsigned int* __restrict path,\n                                     const size_t numNodes,\n                                     const size_t iStart, const size_t iEnd,\n                                     const size_t jStart, const size_t jEnd,\n                                     const size_t kStart, const size_t kEnd) {\n    const size_t jLen = JLEN != 0 ? JLEN : jEnd - jStart;\n\n    for (size_t k = kStart; k < kEnd; ++k) {\n        const unsigned int* __restrict distK = dist + k * numNodes + jStart;\n        const unsigned int kVal = (unsigned int)k;\n        size_t i = iStart;\n\n        for (; i + 1 < iEnd; i += 2) {\n            unsigned int* __restrict distI = dist + i * numNodes;\n            unsigned int* __restrict distI2 = distI + numNodes;\n            const unsigned int distIK = distI[k];\n            const unsigned int distIK2 = distI2[k];\n            unsigned int* __restrict pathI = path + i * numNodes + jStart;\n            unsigned int* __restrict pathI2 = pathI + numNodes;\n            distI += jStart;\n            distI2 += jStart;\n\n            #pragma omp simd\n            for (size_t j = 0; j < jLen; ++j) {\n                const unsigned int distIJ = distI[j];\n                const unsigned int distIJ2 = distI2[j];\n                const unsigned int distKJ = distK[j];\n                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + distKJ;\n                const unsigned int newDist2 = distIK2 + distKJ;\n                const bool better = newDist < distIJ;\n                const bool better2 = newDist2 < distIJ2;\n\n                distI[j] = better ? newDist : distIJ;\n                pathI[j] = better ? kVal : pathI[j];\n                distI2[j] = better2 ? newDist2 : distIJ2;\n                pathI2[j] = better2 ? kVal : pathI2[j];\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (i < iEnd) {\n            unsigned int* __restrict distI = dist + i * numNodes;\n            const unsigned int distIK = distI[k];\n            unsigned int* __restrict pathI = path + i * numNodes + jStart;\n            distI += jStart;\n\n            FW_ROW_UPDATE(distI, pathI, distK, distIK, kVal, jLen)\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Dispatch to the compile time sized kernel whenever the tile is a full block.\nstatic inline void floydWarshallTileDispatch(unsigned int* __restrict dist, unsigned int* __restrict path,\n                                             const size_t numNodes,\n                                             const size_t iStart, const size_t iEnd,\n                                             const size_t jStart, const size_t jEnd,\n                                             const size_t kStart, const size_t kEnd) {\n    if (jEnd - jStart == BLOCK) {\n        floydWarshallTile<BLOCK>(dist, path, numNodes, iStart, iEnd, jStart, jEnd, kStart, kEnd);\n    } else {\n        floydWarshallTile<0>(dist, path, numNodes, iStart, iEnd, jStart, jEnd, kStart, kEnd);\n    }\n}\n\nvoid floydWarshall(unsigned int* dist,\n                   unsigned int* path,\n                   const size_t numNodes) {\n    // Blocked Floyd-Warshall: the k loop is split into blocks and every block\n    // step consists of three phases (diagonal tile, the row/column panels of\n    // the diagonal tile, and all remaining tiles). This computes the same\n    // distance matrix as the classic triple loop, but reuses each tile BLOCK\n    // times while it is in cache and needs only three barriers per block step\n    // instead of one per k.\n    const size_t numBlocks = (numNodes + BLOCK - 1) / BLOCK;\n\n    #pragma omp parallel proc_bind(spread)\n    {\n        for (size_t kb = 0; kb < numBlocks; ++kb) {\n            const size_t kStart = kb * BLOCK;\n            const size_t kEnd = std::min(kStart + BLOCK, numNodes);\n\n            // Phase 1: the diagonal tile depends on itself and is done serially.\n            #pragma omp single\n            {\n                floydWarshallTileDispatch(dist, path, numNodes, kStart, kEnd, kStart, kEnd, kStart, kEnd);\n            }\n\n            // Phase 2: the tiles sharing rows or columns with the diagonal tile.\n            #pragma omp for schedule(static)\n            for (size_t t = 0; t < 2 * numBlocks; ++t) {\n                const size_t b = t >= numBlocks ? t - numBlocks : t;\n                if (b == kb) {\n                    continue;\n                }\n                const size_t bStart = b * BLOCK;\n                const size_t bEnd = std::min(bStart + BLOCK, numNodes);\n\n                if (t < numBlocks) {\n                    // Row panel: tile (kb, b)\n                    floydWarshallTileDispatch(dist, path, numNodes, kStart, kEnd, bStart, bEnd, kStart, kEnd);\n                } else {\n                    // Column panel: tile (b, kb)\n                    floydWarshallTileDispatch(dist, path, numNodes, bStart, bEnd, kStart, kEnd, kStart, kEnd);\n                }\n            }\n\n            // Phase 3: all remaining tiles are independent.\n            #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n            for (size_t ib = 0; ib < numBlocks; ++ib) {\n                for (size_t jb = 0; jb < numBlocks; ++jb) {\n                    if (ib == kb || jb == kb) {\n                        continue;\n                    }\n                    const size_t iStart = ib * BLOCK;\n                    const size_t iEnd = std::min(iStart + BLOCK, numNodes);\n                    const size_t jStart = jb * BLOCK;\n                    const size_t jEnd = std::min(jStart + BLOCK, numNodes);\n\n                    floydWarshallTileDispatch(dist, path, numNodes, iStart, iEnd, jStart, jEnd, kStart, kEnd);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const unsigned int* dist, const size_t numNodes) {\n    // Basic sanity checks\n\n    // 1. Diagonal should be zero\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\\n\", i, i);\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]\n    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {\n                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];\n\n                // Check for overflow before addition\n                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {\n                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {\n                        printf(\"Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\\n\",\n                               i, j, k);\n                        return false;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t numNodes = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Number of nodes: %zu\\n\", numNodes);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    printf(\"OpenMP threads: %d\\n\", omp_get_max_threads());\n\n    // Allocate matrices (uninitialized, so that the parallel first touch below\n    // decides the NUMA placement of the pages)\n    std::unique_ptr<unsigned int[]> distStorage(new unsigned int[numNodes * numNodes]);\n    std::unique_ptr<unsigned int[]> pathStorage(new unsigned int[numNodes * numNodes]);\n    unsigned int* dist = distStorage.get();\n    unsigned int* path = pathStorage.get();\n    firstTouch(dist, numNodes);\n    firstTouch(path, numNodes);\n\n    // Initialize\n    printf(\"Initializing graph...\\n\");\n    initializeDistanceMatrix(dist, numNodes, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);\n    initializePathMatrix(path, numNodes);\n\n    // Run Floyd-Warshall\n    printf(\"Computing shortest paths...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n    // Calculate operations per second\n    // Floyd-Warshall Because every pair (i, j) is then\n// relaxed independently, the loops can be reordered with i outermost, which\n// keeps the target row in L1 while it is relaxed against the whole k block.\n// The (k,j) tile is packed into a contiguous buffer first: reading it in place\n// would walk the matrix with a row stride that is a multiple of 4 KiB for the\n// usual matrix sizes and therefore maps every row onto the same L1 cache sets.\ntemplate <size_t JLEN>\nstatic inline void floydWarshallBlockTile(unsigned int* __restrict dist, unsigned int* __restrict path,\n                                          const size_t numNodes,\n                                          const size_t iStart, const size_t iEnd,\n                                          const size_t jStart, const size_t jEnd,\n                                          const size_t kStart, const size_t kEnd) {\n    const size_t jLen = JLEN != 0 ? JLEN : jEnd - jStart;\n    const size_t kLen = kEnd - kStart;\n\n    alignas(64) unsigned int distKPacked[BLOCK * BLOCK];\n    for (size_t kk = 0; kk < kLen; ++kk) {\n        const unsigned int* __restrict src = dist + (kStart + kk) * numNodes + jStart;\n        unsigned int* __restrict dstRow = distKPacked + kk * jLen;\n        #pragma omp simd\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < jLen; ++j) {\n            dstRow[j] = src[j];\n        }\n    }\n\n    for (size_t i = iStart; i < iEnd; ++i) {\n        unsigned int* __restrict distI = dist + i * numNodes + jStart;\n        unsigned int* __restrict pathI = path + i * numNodes + jStart;\n        const unsigned int* __restrict distIK = dist + i * numNodes + kStart;\n\n        for (size_t kk = 0; kk < kLen; ++kk) {\n            const unsigned int* __restrict distK = distKPacked + kk * jLen;\n            const unsigned int dik = distIK[kk];\n            const unsigned int kVal = (unsigned int)(kStart + kk);\n\n            FW_ROW_UPDATE(distI, pathI, distK, dik, kVal, jLen)\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Dispatch to the compile time sized kernel whenever the tile is a full block.","originalFile":"#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <memory>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <omp.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nconstexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;\nconstexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;\n\n// Index calculation for flattened 2D array\ninline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {\n    return j * n + i;\n}\n\n// The kernel accesses the matrices as rows of length numNodes (dist[idx2(j, i, n)]\n// is element j of row i). Rows are distributed over the threads with a static\n// schedule so that every thread keeps working on the same rows for all k\n// iterations, which maximizes cache reuse and (together with the parallel\n// first-touch initialization below) keeps the data NUMA-local.\nstatic void firstTouch(unsigned int* __restrict data, const size_t numNodes) {\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) proc_bind(spread)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n        unsigned int* __restrict row = data + i * numNodes;\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {\n            row[j] = 0;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nvoid initializeDistanceMatrix(unsigned int* __restrict dist, const size_t numNodes,\n                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;\n\n    // Kept sequential: rand_r carries a serial dependency through its seed, so\n    // this reproduces the exact same graph as the original code.\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes * numNodes; ++i) {\n        dist[i] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);\n    }\n\n    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n        dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] = 0;\n    }\n}\n\nvoid initializePathMatrix(unsigned int* __restrict path, const size_t numNodes) {\n    // The original nested loops assign path[idx2(i, j)] = j and path[idx2(j, i)] = i,\n    // i.e. every element of the row starting at j * numNodes ends up holding j.\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) proc_bind(spread)\n    for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {\n        unsigned int* __restrict row = path + j * numNodes;\n        const unsigned int value = (unsigned int)j;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n            row[i] = value;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Block size of the tiled Floyd-Warshall. A tile of BLOCK x BLOCK unsigned ints\n// is 16 KiB, so the three tiles a block update touches stay resident in L2.\nconstexpr size_t BLOCK = 64;\n\n// Relax all pairs (i, j) of a tile through all intermediate nodes k of a block.\n// The k loop is the outermost one and the updates are done in place, which is\n// exactly the dependency structure the blocked Floyd-Warshall requires (the\n// three tiles may partially or fully overlap).\n// Relax one row against the row of the intermediate node (branch-free so that it\n// vectorizes into compares plus blends). Rows i and k may be the same row, but\n// then dist[k][k] == 0 makes every update a no-op, so the iterations of the loop\n// stay independent.\n#define FW_ROW_UPDATE(distI, pathI, distK, distIK, kVal, jLen)          \\\n    _Pragma(\"omp simd\")                                                 \\\n    for (size_t j = 0; j < (jLen); ++j) {                               \\\n        const unsigned int distIJ = (distI)[j];                         \\\n        const unsigned int newDist = (distIK) + (distK)[j];             \\\n        const bool better = newDist < distIJ;                           \\\n        (distI)[j] = better ? newDist : distIJ;                         \\\n        (pathI)[j] = better ? (kVal) : (pathI)[j];                      \\\n    }\n\n// JLEN is the compile time width of the tile (0 selects the runtime width used\n// for the remainder tiles of a matrix whose size is not a multiple of BLOCK).\n// Two rows i are processed at a time so that the loads of the k row are shared.\ntemplate <size_t JLEN>\nstatic inline void floydWarshallTile(unsigned int* __restrict dist, unsigned int* __restrict path,\n                                     const size_t numNodes,\n                                     const size_t iStart, const size_t iEnd,\n                                     const size_t jStart, const size_t jEnd,\n                                     const size_t kStart, const size_t kEnd) {\n    const size_t jLen = JLEN != 0 ? JLEN : jEnd - jStart;\n\n    for (size_t k = kStart; k < kEnd; ++k) {\n        const unsigned int* __restrict distK = dist + k * numNodes + jStart;\n        const unsigned int kVal = (unsigned int)k;\n        for (size_t i = iStart; i < iEnd; ++i) {\n            unsigned int* __restrict distI = dist + i * numNodes;\n            const unsigned int distIK = distI[k];\n            unsigned int* __restrict pathI = path + i * numNodes + jStart;\n            distI += jStart;\n\n            FW_ROW_UPDATE(distI, pathI, distK, distIK, kVal, jLen)\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Dispatch to the compile time sized kernel whenever the tile is a full block.\nstatic inline void floydWarshallTileDispatch(unsigned int* __restrict dist, unsigned int* __restrict path,\n                                             const size_t numNodes,\n                                             const size_t iStart, const size_t iEnd,\n                                             const size_t jStart, const size_t jEnd,\n                                             const size_t kStart, const size_t kEnd) {\n    if (jEnd - jStart == BLOCK) {\n        floydWarshallTile<BLOCK>(dist, path, numNodes, iStart, iEnd, jStart, jEnd, kStart, kEnd);\n    } else {\n        floydWarshallTile<0>(dist, path, numNodes, iStart, iEnd, jStart, jEnd, kStart, kEnd);\n    }\n}\n\nvoid floydWarshall(unsigned int* dist,\n                   unsigned int* path,\n                   const size_t numNodes) {\n    // Blocked Floyd-Warshall: the k loop is split into blocks and every block\n    // step consists of three phases (diagonal tile, the row/column panels of\n    // the diagonal tile, and all remaining tiles). This computes the same\n    // distance matrix as the classic triple loop, but reuses each tile BLOCK\n    // times while it is in cache and needs only three barriers per block step\n    // instead of one per k.\n    const size_t numBlocks = (numNodes + BLOCK - 1) / BLOCK;\n\n    #pragma omp parallel proc_bind(spread)\n    {\n        for (size_t kb = 0; kb < numBlocks; ++kb) {\n            const size_t kStart = kb * BLOCK;\n            const size_t kEnd = std::min(kStart + BLOCK, numNodes);\n\n            // Phase 1: the diagonal tile depends on itself and is done serially.\n            #pragma omp single\n            {\n                floydWarshallTileDispatch(dist, path, numNodes, kStart, kEnd, kStart, kEnd, kStart, kEnd);\n            }\n\n            // Phase 2: the tiles sharing rows or columns with the diagonal tile.\n            #pragma omp for schedule(static)\n            for (size_t t = 0; t < 2 * numBlocks; ++t) {\n                const size_t b = t >= numBlocks ? t - numBlocks : t;\n                if (b == kb) {\n                    continue;\n                }\n                const size_t bStart = b * BLOCK;\n                const size_t bEnd = std::min(bStart + BLOCK, numNodes);\n\n                if (t < numBlocks) {\n                    // Row panel: tile (kb, b)\n                    floydWarshallTileDispatch(dist, path, numNodes, kStart, kEnd, bStart, bEnd, kStart, kEnd);\n                } else {\n                    // Column panel: tile (b, kb)\n                    floydWarshallTileDispatch(dist, path, numNodes, bStart, bEnd, kStart, kEnd, kStart, kEnd);\n                }\n            }\n\n            // Phase 3: all remaining tiles are independent.\n            #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n            for (size_t ib = 0; ib < numBlocks; ++ib) {\n                for (size_t jb = 0; jb < numBlocks; ++jb) {\n                    if (ib == kb || jb == kb) {\n                        continue;\n                    }\n                    const size_t iStart = ib * BLOCK;\n                    const size_t iEnd = std::min(iStart + BLOCK, numNodes);\n                    const size_t jStart = jb * BLOCK;\n                    const size_t jEnd = std::min(jStart + BLOCK, numNodes);\n\n                    floydWarshallTileDispatch(dist, path, numNodes, iStart, iEnd, jStart, jEnd, kStart, kEnd);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const unsigned int* dist, const size_t numNodes) {\n    // Basic sanity checks\n\n    // 1. Diagonal should be zero\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\\n\", i, i);\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]\n    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {\n                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];\n\n                // Check for overflow before addition\n                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {\n                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {\n                        printf(\"Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\\n\",\n                               i, j, k);\n                        return false;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t numNodes = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Number of nodes: %zu\\n\", numNodes);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    printf(\"OpenMP threads: %d\\n\", omp_get_max_threads());\n\n    // Allocate matrices (uninitialized, so that the parallel first touch below\n    // decides the NUMA placement of the pages)\n    std::unique_ptr<unsigned int[]> Rows i and k may be the same row, but\n// then dist[k][k] == 0 makes every update a no-op, so the iterations of the loop\n// stay independent.\n#define FW_ROW_UPDATE(distI, pathI, distK, distIK, kVal, jLen)          \\\n    _Pragma(\"omp simd\")                                                 \\\n    for (size_t j = 0; j < (jLen); ++j) {                               \\\n        const unsigned int distIJ = (distI)[j];                         \\\n        const unsigned int newDist = (distIK) + (distK)[j];             \\\n        const bool better = newDist < distIJ;                           \\\n        (distI)[j] = better ? newDist : distIJ;                         \\\n        (pathI)[j] = better ? Because every pair (i, j) is then\n// relaxed independently, the loops can be reordered with i outermost, which\n// keeps the target row in L1 while it is relaxed against the whole k block.\n// The (k,j) tile is packed into a contiguous buffer first: reading it in place\n// would walk the matrix with a row stride that is a multiple of 4 KiB for the\n// usual matrix sizes and therefore maps every row onto the same L1 cache sets.\ntemplate <size_t JLEN>\nstatic inline void floydWarshallBlockTile(unsigned int* __restrict dist, unsigned int* __restrict path,\n                                          const size_t numNodes,\n                                          const size_t iStart, const size_t iEnd,\n                                          const size_t jStart, const size_t jEnd,\n                                          const size_t kStart, const size_t kEnd) {\n    const size_t jLen = JLEN != 0 ? kk < kLen; ++kk) {\n            const unsigned int* __restrict distK = distKPacked + kk * jLen;\n            const unsigned int dik = distIK[kk];\n            const unsigned int kVal = (unsigned int)(kStart + kk);\n\n            FW_ROW_UPDATE(distI, pathI, distK, dik, kVal, jLen)\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Dispatch to the compile time sized kernel whenever the tile is a full block.\nstatic inline void floydWarshallTileDispatch(unsigned int* __restrict dist, unsigned int* __restrict path,\n                                             const size_t numNodes,\n                                             const size_t iStart, const size_t iEnd,\n                                             const size_t jStart, const size_t jEnd,\n                                             const size_t kStart, const size_t kEnd) {\n    if (jEnd - jStart == BLOCK) {\n        floydWarshallTile<BLOCK>(dist, path, numNodes, iStart, iEnd, jStart, jEnd, kStart, kEnd);\n    } else {\n        floydWarshallTile<0>(dist, path, numNodes, iStart, iEnd, jStart, jEnd, kStart, kEnd);\n    }\n}\n\nvoid floydWarshall(unsigned int* dist,\n                   unsigned int* path,\n                   const size_t numNodes) {\n    // Blocked Floyd-Warshall: the k loop is split into blocks and every block\n    // step consists of three phases (diagonal tile, the row/column panels of\n    // the diagonal tile, and all remaining tiles). This computes the same\n    // distance matrix as the classic triple loop, but reuses each tile BLOCK\n    // times while it is in cache and needs only three barriers per block step\n    // instead of one per k.\n    const size_t numBlocks = (numNodes + BLOCK - 1) / BLOCK;\n\n    #pragma omp parallel proc_bind(spread)\n    {\n        for (size_t kb = 0; kb < numBlocks; ++kb) {\n            const size_t kStart = kb * BLOCK;\n            const size_t kEnd = std::min(kStart + BLOCK, numNodes);\n\n            // Phase 1: the diagonal tile depends on itself and is done serially.\n            #pragma omp single\n            {\n                floydWarshallTileDispatch(dist, path, numNodes, kStart, kEnd, kStart, kEnd, kStart, kEnd);\n            }\n\n            // Phase 2: the tiles sharing rows or columns with the diagonal tile.\n            #pragma omp for schedule(static)\n            for (size_t t = 0; t < 2 * numBlocks; ++t) {\n                const size_t b = t >= numBlocks ? t - numBlocks : t;\n                if (b == kb) {\n                    continue;\n                }\n                const size_t bStart = b * BLOCK;\n                const size_t bEnd = std::min(bStart + BLOCK, numNodes);\n\n                if (t < numBlocks) {\n                    // Row panel: tile (kb, b)\n                    floydWarshallTileDispatch(dist, path, numNodes, kStart, kEnd, bStart, bEnd, kStart, kEnd);\n                } else {\n                    // Column panel: tile (b, kb)\n                    floydWarshallTileDispatch(dist, path, numNodes, bStart, bEnd, kStart, kEnd, kStart, kEnd);\n                }\n            }\n\n            // Phase 3: all remaining tiles are independent.\n            #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n            for (size_t ib = 0; ib < numBlocks; ++ib) {\n                for (size_t jb = 0; jb < numBlocks; ++jb) {\n                    if (ib == kb || jb == kb) {\n                        continue;\n                    }\n                    const size_t iStart = ib * BLOCK;\n                    const size_t iEnd = std::min(iStart + BLOCK, numNodes);\n                    const size_t jStart = jb * BLOCK;\n                    const size_t jEnd = std::min(jStart + BLOCK, numNodes);\n\n                    floydWarshallTileDispatch(dist, path, numNodes, iStart, iEnd, jStart, jEnd, kStart, kEnd);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const unsigned int* dist, const size_t numNodes) {\n    // Basic sanity checks\n\n    // 1. Diagonal should be zero\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\\n\", i, i);\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]\n    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {\n                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];\n\n                // Check for overflow before addition\n                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {\n                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {\n                        printf(\"Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\\n\",\n                               i, j, k);\n                        return false;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t numNodes = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Number of nodes: %zu\\n\", numNodes);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    printf(\"OpenMP threads: %d\\n\", omp_get_max_threads());\n\n    // Allocate matrices (uninitialized, so that the parallel first touch below\n    // decides the NUMA placement of the pages)\n    std::unique_ptr<unsigned int[]> distStorage(new unsigned int[numNodes * numNodes]);\n    std::unique_ptr<unsigned int[]> pathStorage(new unsigned int[numNodes * numNodes]);\n    unsigned int* dist = distStorage.get();\n    unsigned int* path = pathStorage.get();\n    firstTouch(dist, numNodes);\n    firstTouch(path, numNodes);\n\n    // Initialize\n    printf(\"Initializing graph...\\n\");\n    initializeDistanceMatrix(dist, numNodes, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);\n    initializePathMatrix(path, numNodes);\n\n    // Run Floyd-Warshall\n    printf(\"Computing shortest paths...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    floydWarshall(dist, path, numNodes);\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n    // Calculate operations per second\n    // Floyd-Warshall has O(n³) complexity\n    double ops = (double)numNodes * numNodes * numNodes;\n    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f GOPS\\n\", gflops);\n\n    // Print results for external validation (integer hash-based)\n    if (printResults) {\n        const std::vector<unsigned int> distVec(dist, dist + numNodes * numNodes);\n        print_results_int(distVec, \"DistanceMatrix\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        bool valid = validateResult(dist, numNodes);\n\n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return 0;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":165,"oldLines":6,"newStart":165,"newLines":18,"lines":["     }"," }"," ","+static inline void floydWarshallBlockTileDispatch(unsigned int* The diagonal tile depends on itself,\n// the row panel additionally reads the diagonal tile as source of dist[i][k],\n// and the column panel reads it as source of dist[k][j].\nenum TileKind { TILE_DIAGONAL, TILE_ROW_PANEL, TILE_COLUMN_PANEL };\n\n// Kernel for the tiles of phase 1 and 2, which overlap with the diagonal tile\n// and therefore have to keep the k loop outermost. The tiles are copied into\n// contiguous scratch buffers first, because in the matrix itself the rows of a\n// tile are a multiple of 4 KiB apart for the usual matrix sizes and would all\n// map onto the same L1 cache sets. JLEN : jEnd - jStart;\n    const size_t kLen = kEnd - kStart;\n\n    alignas(64) unsigned int tileDist[BLOCK * BLOCK];\n    alignas(64) unsigned int tilePath[BLOCK * BLOCK];\n    alignas(64) unsigned int tileOther[BLOCK * BLOCK];\n\n    for (size_t ii = 0; ii < iLen; ++ii) {\n        const unsigned int* __restrict srcDist = dist + (iStart + ii) * numNodes + jStart;\n        const unsigned int* __restrict srcPath = path + (iStart + ii) * numNodes + jStart;\n        #pragma omp simd\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < jLen; ++j) {\n            tileDist[ii * BLOCK + j] = srcDist[j];\n            tilePath[ii * BLOCK + j] = srcPath[j];\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (KIND == TILE_ROW_PANEL) {\n        // dist[i][k] comes from the diagonal tile (rows iStart.., columns kStart..)\n        for (size_t ii = 0; ii < iLen; ++ii) {\n            const unsigned int* __restrict src = dist + (iStart + ii) * numNodes + kStart;\n            #pragma omp simd\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < kLen; ++k) {\n                tileOther[ii * BLOCK + k] = src[k];\n            }\n        }\n    } else if (KIND == TILE_COLUMN_PANEL) {\n        // dist[k][j] comes from the diagonal tile (rows kStart.., columns jStart..)\n        for (size_t kk = 0; kk < kLen; ++kk) {\n            const unsigned int* __restrict src = dist + (kStart + kk) * numNodes + jStart;\n            #pragma omp simd\n            for (size_t j = 0; j < jLen; ++j) {\n                tileOther[kk * BLOCK + j] = src[j];\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    for (size_t kk = 0; kk < kLen; ++kk) {\n        const unsigned int* __restrict distK =\n            (KIND == TILE_COLUMN_PANEL ?
